# Skills · F · part 14

894 entries, figure-generation to filing-gate-review. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **figure-generation**: Use when making or restyling journal figures from analysis results. Triggers on "画图"、"出图"、"投稿图"、"生存曲线"、"森林图"、"make a figure"、"KM plot"、"forest plot"、"journal figure format". ([Stefansong/med-research-powers](https://github.com/Stefansong/med-research-powers/tree/HEAD/skills/figure-generation) · ★4)
- **figure-generation**: How to render a publication-ready figure in a Findings Workflow project: read the color registry, apply the publication defaults, dual-export (SVG + 300 DPI PNG), render a separate legend image, and handle the &gt;8-category problem. Use whenever generating a figure for a finding or report. ([mriffle/findings-ai-collab-workflow](https://github.com/mriffle/findings-ai-collab-workflow/tree/HEAD/skills/figure-generation) · ★2)
- **figure-generator**: Generates figures from placeholders in .tex files. Creates Python matplotlib scripts for data figures, TikZ/Python schematics for diagrams. Maintains a shared plot_defaults.py for consistent styling. Flags complex figures for the user. Runs after the writer skill. ([ccam80/thesis-writer](https://github.com/ccam80/thesis-writer/tree/HEAD/dist/claude/thesis-writer/skills/figure-generator) · ★6 · 2 copies)
- **figure-generator**: Generate publication-style charts from structured data. Use when the user wants a figure, chart, or visualization for research results. ([kenantang/codex-and-claude-skills](https://github.com/kenantang/codex-and-claude-skills/tree/HEAD/collected-academic-research-skills/sources/ymx10086__ResearchClaw/src/researchclaw/agents/skills/figure_generator))
- **figure-ground**: El cerebro separa elementos en primer plano y fondo. Use cuando diseñe modales, overlays, focus states, o jerarquía visual de capas. ([vjrivmon/Setup-Software-IA](https://github.com/vjrivmon/Setup-Software-IA/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ux-ui/gestalt/figure-ground) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **figure-ground-cues**: Apply specific visual cues to control figure-ground perception — shadow, elevation, scale, contrast, saturation, opacity, and texture. Use when designing layered interfaces, focus states, hover treatments, or any context where one element should clearly stand forward against others. Each cue contributes a portion of the figure-ground signal; combining cues strengthens the effect. The skill is selecting and calibrating cues for the desired level of separation. ([Deibler/universal-design-principles](https://github.com/Deibler/universal-design-principles/tree/HEAD/plugins/perception-and-hierarchy-principles/skills/figure-ground-cues) · ★8)
- **figure-ground-overlays-and-modals**: Apply figure-ground separation specifically for overlays — modals, dialogs, drawers, popovers, dropdowns, toasts. Use when designing or auditing the moments when a UI element appears over the underlying page and needs to clearly come forward. The most common figure-ground design problem; getting it right means users naturally focus on the overlay, getting it wrong means modals blur into the page or feel jarring. ([Deibler/universal-design-principles](https://github.com/Deibler/universal-design-principles/tree/HEAD/plugins/perception-and-hierarchy-principles/skills/figure-ground-overlays-and-modals) · ★8)
- **figure-ground-relationship**: Apply the Gestalt principle of Figure-Ground Relationship — the perceptual separation of what's perceived as the figure (the focus, the object) from the ground (the surrounding context). Use when designing modals, overlays, focus states, hero elements, hover treatments, layered interfaces, or any context where one element should clearly stand forward against everything else. The user's perception always sorts the visual field into figure and ground; designs that reinforce the desired sorting feel clear, while designs that fight it feel confusing. ([Deibler/universal-design-principles](https://github.com/Deibler/universal-design-principles/tree/HEAD/plugins/perception-and-hierarchy-principles/skills/figure-ground-relationship) · ★8)
- **figure-guide**: 이미지 생성 프롬프트 작성 가이드 - 프롬프트 가이드, 캡션 패턴, 예시 프롬프트 통합 ([orientpine/honeypot](https://github.com/orientpine/honeypot/tree/HEAD/plugins/isd-generator/skills/figure-guide) · ★35)
- **figure-heatmap**: Create heatmaps, contour plots, and density of states (DOS/PDOS) figures for publication. Use when the user asks to create "heatmap", "히트맵", "contour plot", "등고선", "DOS", "PDOS", "density of states", "상태밀도", "correlation matrix", "상관 행렬", "activity map", "volcano plot", or any 2D color-mapped visualization for their paper. Also trigger on "descriptor map", "에너지 맵", "2D plot". ([woonsuk-yeo/claude-research-skills](https://github.com/woonsuk-yeo/claude-research-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/4-figure-factory/skills/figure-heatmap))
- **figure-helper**: Generates and edits TikZ / PGFPlots figures from a description or data. Produces correct preamble (\\usepackage{tikz}, \\usepackage{pgfplots}, \\pgfplotsset{compat=newest}), uses canonical TikZ libraries, and supplies the right styling defaults (axis labels, gridlines, legend, mark choice). Use when the user asks for a TikZ figure, a labeled plot, a state diagram, a flowchart, a PGFPlots chart from data, or wants to convert a description into a figure. Delegates to /academic-latex:docs-lookup for non-trivial TikZ/PGFPlots options. ([SeidSmatti/misc-CC-Plugins](https://github.com/SeidSmatti/misc-CC-Plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/academic-latex/skills/figure-helper))
- **figure-integrity**: Produce figures that are real, readable, and actually referenced. Use when generating, inserting, resizing, or debugging figures in a paper, and when a vision model rejects an image. Covers data honesty, the JPEG-named-PNG failure that kills whole runs, upscaling, and dense bitmaps crushed into a column. ([JihaoXin/paper-machine](https://github.com/JihaoXin/paper-machine/tree/HEAD/skills/figure-integrity))

- **figure-it-out**: Explore and debate options with fresh documentation and research before committing. Use when facing a real decision with multiple plausible approaches — library/framework choice, architecture call, API or schema design, algorithm selection, or any communication / strategy call where being wrong has cost. Enumerates relevant research domains, looks up current docs and evidence-based methods, weighs options on correctness and elegance, resists bloat. Do NOT use for divergent ideation (brainstorm), extracting user intent (elicit), or reviewing already-written code (quality-review). ([TheMostlyGreat/mythos](https://github.com/TheMostlyGreat/mythos/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/figure-it-out) · ★19)

- **figure-legend-gen**: Generate standardized figure legends for scientific figures and charts; use when preparing publication-ready legends that summarize design, variables, sample size, and key statistical notes. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Academic%20Writing/figure-legend-gen) · ★1.9k)
- **figure-legend-gen**: Generate standardized figure legends for scientific charts and graphs. Trigger when user uploads/requesting legend for research figures, academic papers, or data charts. Supports bar charts, line graphs, scatter plots, box plots, heatmaps, and microscopy images. This tool generates text legends only, not visualizations. - **figure-quality-review-gate**: Hard review gate for scientific R/ggplot figures before merge or release.
